,"content":"My apologies! The JNI wrapper was aggressively rejecting COM initialization if the calling Java thread was 
already initialized using a Single-Threaded Apartment (STA) model instead of a Multi-Threaded Apartment (MTA). 
\n\nBecause Java UI frameworks frequently initialize their own COM threads (and often default to STA), 
`CoInitializeEx` was returning `RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE`. Previously, the code ignored this and worked fine, but my strict 
`ComInit` wrapper was treating it as a total failure and bailing out of the `switchDesktop` and `moveWindowToDesktop` 
JNI calls entirely!\n\nI've pushed a fix that allows `ComInit` to gracefully accept `RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE` as a success 
state (since it just means the thread is already fully capable of handling COM objects). \n\nThe C++ code has been 
successfully recompiled. Try it now—switching and moving to virtual desktops should be fully functional

:1359:{"step_index":1374,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-05-28T14:05:30Z"
,"content":"Ah, I see what's happening! The Windows Shell COM API for virtual desktops operates **asynchronously** 
behind the scenes. \n\nWhen you ask it to `switchDesktop`, it fires off the command to the Shell and returns success 
almost immediately while the animation plays. However, the custom `ComInit` RAII wrapper I added to those functions 
was strictly calling `CoUninitialize()` the moment the JNI method ended. This completely destroyed the COM apartment 
on that thread before the Shell had a chance to actually finish switching the desktops, effectively aborting the 
action!\n\nThe older version worked because it intentionally \"leaked\" the `CoInitializeEx` on the Java thread, 
keeping the apartment alive indefinitely and allowing the async Shell COM actions to fully complete in the background. 
\n\nI've reverted those specific methods (`switchDesktop`, `moveWindowToDesktop`, `enumerateDesktops`, 
`getCurrentDesktopId`, etc) back to use the older `CoInitializeEx(nullptr, COINIT_MULTITHREADED);` without tearing it 
down. \n\nI just rebuilt the DLL. Give it a test now—switching and moving to virtual desktops should be working 
perfectly just like they did in the older version!","thinking":"**Prioritizing Tool Usage**\n\nI'm now focusing on 
tool selection, emphasizing specific tools over general ones where possible. The goal is to optimize efficiency by 
leveraging tools tailored to particular tasks, like viewing files. This strategy promises a more targeted and less 
error-prone approach to problem-solving, which I will continue to refine and implement moving forward.\n\n\n"}
